# Breweries for BeerKeeper
#
# One brewery per line: either just a name, or "Name | https://website.com"
# if you want a website attached. Blank lines and lines starting with "#"
# are ignored.
#
# This file seeds the breweries TABLE, once, the first time the app boots
# with an empty database - these become real Brewery rows from that point
# on and are managed like any other brewery: rename, delete, or add more
# through the app itself. Editing this file after first boot has no
# effect; it's only a seed, not a live source. See the README for
# details.
#
# Two sources make up this list:
# 1. A hand-picked starting set compiled from public sources (mainly a mix
#    of Wikipedia and craft-beer press coverage, cross-checked where
#    possible) - listed first in each country section.
# 2. Bulk additions from Open Brewery DB (https://www.openbrewerydb.org/),
#    an open-source, community-maintained dataset, MIT licensed - listed
#    under an "Open Brewery DB additions" marker in each section. Limited
#    to currently-operating breweries (closed and planning locations, plus
#    bars/taprooms/beer gardens with no brewing of their own, are excluded).
#    Where a hand-picked entry and an Open Brewery DB entry were clearly
#    the same brewery (e.g. "Co." vs "Co"), the hand-picked one was
#    removed in favor of Open Brewery DB's. Near-duplicates within Open
#    Brewery DB itself (e.g. "Big Storm Brewing" vs "Big Storm Brewing
#    Co.") were reviewed individually and merged into the more complete
#    name where the match seemed clear; a handful judged too ambiguous
#    (e.g. a possible separate "...Brewpub" location) were left as-is.
#    Two different, unrelated breweries that happen to share an exact
#    name across countries can still both exist here - only the first
#    one seeded actually becomes a row, since names must be unique.
#
# Neither source is an exhaustive or official registry, and brewery details
# (name spelling, active status, websites) can drift over time. It's just a
# helpful starting point; add, remove, or fix entries freely through the app
# once it's running.
